\seq_gput_right:Nn \g_ftoo_evidence_tags_seq { ev:decay-occ }
\prop_gput:Nnn \g_ftoo_evidence_tag_to_id_prop { ev:decay-occ } { sem:evidence:decay-supports-occupancy }
\prop_gput:Nnn \g_ftoo_evidence_source_prop { ev:decay-occ } { sem:parameter:free-mean-decay-rate }
\prop_gput:Nnn \g_ftoo_evidence_target_prop { ev:decay-occ } { sem:claim:occupancy }
\prop_gput:Nnn \g_ftoo_evidence_force_prop { ev:decay-occ } { the~APPLICABILITY~of~occupancy,~not~the~will's~existence:~neglected~edges~actually~decay,~so~occupancy~is~not~vacuous~in~a~zero-decay~world }

\seq_gput_right:Nn \g_ftoo_evidence_tags_seq { ev:knee-rate }
\prop_gput:Nnn \g_ftoo_evidence_tag_to_id_prop { ev:knee-rate } { sem:evidence:knee-supports-rate }
\prop_gput:Nnn \g_ftoo_evidence_source_prop { ev:knee-rate } { sem:claim:release-knee }
\prop_gput:Nnn \g_ftoo_evidence_target_prop { ev:knee-rate } { sem:claim:will-is-a-rate }
\prop_gput:Nnn \g_ftoo_evidence_force_prop { ev:knee-rate } { the~will's~TYPE~alone:~no~knee~at~resolution~bounds~a~reservoir's~time-constant,~which~is~the~rate~reading~and~not~the~bundled~posit }

\seq_gput_right:Nn \g_ftoo_evidence_tags_seq { ev:resid-ex }

\prop_gput:Nnn \g_ftoo_evidence_source_prop { ev:resid-ex } { sem:claim:will-identification }
\prop_gput:Nnn \g_ftoo_evidence_target_prop { ev:resid-ex } { sem:claim:will-exists }
\prop_gput:Nnn \g_ftoo_evidence_force_prop { ev:resid-ex } { EXISTENCE:~a~nonzero~identified~residual,~where~edges~are~held~below~self-sustainment~and~zero~in~a~will-free~world }

\seq_gput_right:Nn \g_ftoo_evidence_tags_seq { ev:resid-opp }
\prop_gput:Nnn \g_ftoo_evidence_tag_to_id_prop { ev:resid-opp } { sem:evidence:residual-supports-opposition }
\prop_gput:Nnn \g_ftoo_evidence_source_prop { ev:resid-opp } { sem:claim:will-identification }
\prop_gput:Nnn \g_ftoo_evidence_target_prop { ev:resid-opp } { sem:claim:will-opposes-reward }
\prop_gput:Nnn \g_ftoo_evidence_force_prop { ev:resid-opp } { DIRECTION,~a~separate~warrant~from~existence:~the~residual~space~is~signed,~so~opposition~to~the~reward~gradient~is~a~finding~the~field~could~fail }

\prop_gput:Nnn \g_ftoo_evidence_force_prop { ev:afriat-conv } { the~epistemic~posit:~rationalization~recovers~every~spent~gradient~and~nothing~unspent }

\seq_gput_right:Nn \g_ftoo_evidence_tags_seq { ev:tremble-conv }
